#### ðŸ“ `C:\EAR_OS_V2\src\modules\SClassScreens\components\SClassUI.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { AlertTriangle, LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';

export const BentoCard = ({ title, subtitle, children, danger = false }: any) => (
    <div className={`bg-white/5 border ${danger ? 'border-red-500/30 hover:border-red-500/60' : 'border-white/10 hover:border-ear-gold/30'} rounded-[2.5rem] p-8 backdrop-blur-xl relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col group transition-all duration-500 hover:shadow-2xl hover:shadow-ear-gold/5 hover:-translate-y-1`}>
        {danger && <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-red-500/50 to-transparent animate-pulse" />}
        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-6">
            <div>
                <h3 className={`text-xl font-black uppercase italic tracking-tighter ${danger ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-white'} group-hover:tracking-normal transition-all duration-500`}>{title}</h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest mt-1 italic">{subtitle}</p>
            </div>
            {danger && <AlertTriangle size={14} className="text-red-500 animate-pulse" />}
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 relative z-10">
            {children}
        </div>
    </div>
);

export const StatBox = ({ label, value, color = "text-white", className = "" }: any) => (
    <div className={`text-right group cursor-default ${className}`}>
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 group-hover:text-ear-gold transition-colors">{label}</p>
        <p className={`${color} text-2xl font-black italic`}>{value}</p>
    </div>
);

export const LogEntry = ({ time, type, msg, color = "text-gray-400" }: any) => (
    <div className="flex gap-4 items-center group py-1 border-b border-white/[0.02] last:border-0 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider">
        <span className="text-gray-600 w-12 shrink-0">{time}</span>
        <span className={`w-10 font-black shrink-0 ${color}`}>[{type}]</span>
        <span className="text-gray-300 group-hover:text-white transition-colors truncate">{msg}</span>
    </div>
);

export const SmallKPI = ({ icon: Icon, label, value }: { icon: LucideIcon, label: string, value: string }) => (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4 bg-black/20 border border-white/5 rounded-2xl">
        <Icon size={16} className="text-ear-gold" />
        <div>
            <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-gray-600 leading-none mb-1">{label}</p>
            <p className="text-xs font-black text-white italic leading-none">{value}</p>
        </div>
    </div>
);
```

#### ðŸ“ `C:\EAR_OS_V2\src\modules\SClassScreens\components\SClassUI.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { AlertTriangle, LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';

export const BentoCard = ({ title, subtitle, children, danger = false }: any) => (
    <div className={`bg-white/5 border ${danger ? 'border-red-500/30 hover:border-red-500/60' : 'border-white/10 hover:border-ear-gold/30'} rounded-[2.5rem] p-8 backdrop-blur-xl relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col group transition-all duration-500 hover:shadow-2xl hover:shadow-ear-gold/5 hover:-translate-y-1`}>
        {danger && <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-red-500/50 to-transparent animate-pulse" />}
        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-6">
            <div>
                <h3 className={`text-xl font-black uppercase italic tracking-tighter ${danger ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-white'} group-hover:tracking-normal transition-all duration-500`}>{title}</h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest mt-1 italic">{subtitle}</p>
            </div>
            {danger && <AlertTriangle size={14} className="text-red-500 animate-pulse" />}
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 relative z-10">
            {children}
        </div>
    </div>
);

export const StatBox = ({ label, value, color = "text-white", className = "" }: any) => (
    <div className={`text-right group cursor-default ${className}`}>
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 group-hover:text-ear-gold transition-colors">{label}</p>
        <p className={`${color} text-2xl font-black italic`}>{value}</p>
    </div>
);

export const LogEntry = ({ time, type, msg, color = "text-gray-400" }: any) => (
    <div className="flex gap-4 items-center group py-1 border-b border-white/[0.02] last:border-0 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider">
        <span className="text-gray-600 w-12 shrink-0">{time}</span>
        <span className={`w-10 font-black shrink-0 ${color}`}>[{type}]</span>
        <span className="text-gray-300 group-hover:text-white transition-colors truncate">{msg}</span>
    </div>
);

export const SmallKPI = ({ icon: Icon, label, value }: { icon: LucideIcon, label: string, value: string }) => (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4 bg-black/20 border border-white/5 rounded-2xl">
        <Icon size={16} className="text-ear-gold" />
        <div>
            <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-gray-600 leading-none mb-1">{label}</p>
            <p className="text-xs font-black text-white italic leading-none">{value}</p>
        </div>
    </div>
);
```

Comandante, he identificado el fallo estructural en el motor de ejecuciÃ³n del Hunter. 

El problema es un **shadowing** (sombreado) de variables: en la lÃ­nea 36 estÃ¡ declarando una constante llamada `process` para capturar el resultado de `exec`. Debido al funcionamiento de los alcances (scopes) en JavaScript/TypeScript, esta declaraciÃ³n "anula" el acceso al objeto global de Node.js `process` en todo el bloque de la funciÃ³n, incluso en la lÃ­nea 26 donde intenta usar `process.cwd()`.

Para mantener el rigor de Silicon Valley y asegurar que el sistema sea robusto, debemos renombrar la instancia del proceso hijo.

AquÃ­ tiene el cÃ³digo corregido para que lo implemente en `src/app/api/hunter/execute/route.ts`:

### Protocolo de ResoluciÃ³n:

1.  **Cambiar el nombre de la variable**: Renombraremos la instancia de `exec` a `childProcess`.
2.  **Referencia Global**: Esto permitirÃ¡ que `process.cwd()` apunte correctamente al objeto global de Node.js.

```typescript
// ... (resto de importaciones)

export async function POST(req: Request) {
    try {
        const { script } = await req.json();

        if (!WHITELIST_SCRIPTS.includes(script)) {
            return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Script no autorizado' }, { status: 403 });
        }

        // Ahora process.cwd() funcionarÃ¡ correctamente al no estar sombreado
        const scriptPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'src/lib/services/scrapers', script);

        if (!fs.existsSync(scriptPath)) {
            return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Script no encontrado en el sistema' }, { status: 404 });
        }

        console.log(`ðŸš€ IGNICIÃ“N: Ejecutando ${script}...`);

        // Renombramos la variable a childProcess para evitar el conflicto
        const childProcess = exec(`python "${scriptPath}"`, (error, stdout, stderr) => {
            if (error) {
                console.error(`âŒ Error al ejecutar ${script}:`, error);
                return;
            }
            console.log(`âœ… ${script} completado.`);
        });

        return NextResponse.json({ 
            success: true, 
            message: `Protocolo ${script} iniciado en segundo plano.`,
            pid: childProcess.pid // Usamos el nuevo nombre aquÃ­ tambiÃ©n
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error('âŒ HUNTER_EXECUTION_ERROR:', error);
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Error interno del motor de ejecuciÃ³n' }, { status: 500 });
    }
}
```

export async function POST(req: Request) {

        }

        console.log(`ðŸš€ IGNICIÃ“N: Ejecutando ${script}...`);

        // Renombramos la variable a childProcess para evitar el conflicto
        const childProcess = exec(`python "${scriptPath}"`, (error, stdout, stderr) => {
            if (error) {
                console.error(`âŒ Error al ejecutar ${script}:`, error);
                return;
            }
            console.log(`âœ… ${script} completado.`);
        });

        return NextResponse.json({ 
            success: true, 
            message: `Protocolo ${script} iniciado en segundo plano.`,
            pid: childProcess.pid // Usamos el nuevo nombre aquÃ­ tambiÃ©n
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error('âŒ HUNTER_EXECUTION_ERROR:', error);
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Error interno del motor de ejecuciÃ³n' }, { status: 500 });
    }
}

"use client";

import React from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { 
  Zap, 
  Menu, 
  Bell, 
  Sparkles, 
  Calendar, 
  MessageCircle, 
  User, 
  Search,
  CheckCircle2,
  Lock,
  ArrowRight,
  TrendingUp,
  Activity,
  ChartBar,
  ShieldCheck,
  Settings,
  LayoutGrid
} from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { cn } from "@/lib_saas/utils";

/**
 * ðŸ›°ï¸ MODULE: THE SIGNAL DASHBOARD (EAR-OS v3.0)
 * High-End Artist Ecosystem.
 * Implementation based on RAW_STITCH_EXPORT.html
 */

export default function TheSignal() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = React.useState("DASH");

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#102216] dark:bg-[#102216] font-sans text-slate-100 antialiased min-h-screen selection:bg-[#0df259] selection:text-black overflow-x-hidden">
      
      {/* --- App Frame --- */}
      <div className="relative flex min-h-screen w-full flex-col max-w-md mx-auto bg-[#102216] shadow-[0_0_100px_rgba(0,0,0,0.8)] border-x border-white/5">
        
        {/* --- Header --- */}
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 flex items-center bg-[#102216]/80 backdrop-blur-md p-4 pb-2 justify-between border-b border-white/5">
          <button className="text-white hover:text-[#0df259] transition-colors flex size-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full active:bg-white/5">
            <Menu className="w-8 h-8" />
          </button>
          
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
            <h2 className="text-white text-[10px] font-black leading-tight tracking-[0.4em] text-center uppercase">The Signal</h2>
            <div className="h-0.5 w-12 bg-[#0df259] mt-1 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(13,242,89,0.5)] rounded-full"></div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex w-12 items-center justify-end">
            <button className="relative flex size-12 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-transparent text-white hover:text-[#0df259] transition-colors">
              <Bell className="w-6 h-6" />
              <span className="absolute top-3 right-3 h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-[#0df259] shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(13,242,89,0.5)]"></span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* --- Main Content --- */}
        <main className="flex-1 flex flex-col gap-6 p-4 pb-32 overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar">
          
          {/* User Status */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[#0df259] text-[8px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.3em] mb-1">Operator ID: <span className="text-white">KPR-09</span></p>
              <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight uppercase">
                Welcome back, <br/>
                <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-[#0df259] to-white italic">Ghost</span>
              </h1>
            </div>
            <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-full border border-[#0df259]/30 p-1 bg-black/40">
              <img 
                alt="Cyberpunk Avatar" 
                className="h-full w-full rounded-full object-cover grayscale brightness-90 hover:grayscale-0 transition-all duration-300"
                src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1614850523459-c2f4c699c52e?q=80&w=2070&auto=format&fit=crop"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Key Metrics Grid */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <MetricCard 
              label="Legacy Score" 
              value="845" 
              trend="+2.4% cycle" 
              icon={<Zap className="text-[#0df259]/40" size={12} />}
            />
            <MetricCard 
              label="Sys Health" 
              value="92%" 
              trend="Stable" 
              icon={<ShieldCheck className="text-[#0df259]/40" size={12} />}
              isPercent
            />
          </div>

          {/* Dynamic Cache Flow Graph Mockup */}
          <section className="bg-white/5 backdrop-blur-md rounded-2xl p-5 border border-white/5 relative overflow-hidden group hover:border-[#0df259]/20 transition-all">
            <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-[#0df259]/50 to-transparent opacity-50"></div>
            
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <ChartBar className="text-[#0df259] w-3 h-3" />
                  <p className="text-slate-500 text-[8px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">Dynamic Cache Flow</p>
                </div>
                <p className="text-white text-3xl font-black font-mono tracking-tighter">24.5k<span className="text-xs text-[#0df259] ml-1">IPU</span></p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-[#0df259]/10 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-[#0df259]/20">
                <TrendingUp className="text-[#0df259] w-3 h-3" />
                <span className="text-[#0df259] text-[10px] font-black font-mono">+12%</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Graph Visualization (Simplified SVG) */}
            <div className="h-24 w-full opacity-60">
              <svg className="w-full h-full" viewBox="0 0 100 20" preserveAspectRatio="none">
                <defs>
                  <linearGradient id="wave" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
                    <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#0df259" stopOpacity="0.3" />
                    <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#0df259" stopOpacity="0" />
                  </linearGradient>
                </defs>
                <path d="M0,10 Q10,2 20,12 T40,5 T60,15 T80,8 T100,2 V20 H0 Z" fill="url(#wave)" />
                <path d="M0,10 Q10,2 20,12 T40,5 T60,15 T80,8 T100,2" fill="none" stroke="#0df259" strokeWidth="0.5" />
              </svg>
            </div>
            
            <div className="flex justify-between mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-white/5 opacity-30 text-[8px] font-mono font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">
               <span>M</span><span>T</span><span className="text-white">W</span><span>T</span><span>F</span><span>S</span><span>S</span>
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* Upcoming Matches */}
          <section className="space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-1">
              <h2 className="text-white text-lg font-black uppercase tracking-tight">Upcoming Matches</h2>
              <button className="text-[#0df259] text-[8px] font-black tracking-widest uppercase hover:underline">View Intelligence</button>
            </div>
            
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <MatchCard 
                title="Neon Corp Gig" 
                time="Tonight, 22:00" 
                img="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1549490349-8643362247b5?q=80&w=2070&auto=format&fit=crop" 
                status="ACCEPT" 
              />
              <MatchCard 
                title="Sector 7 Collab" 
                time="Tomorrow, 14:00" 
                img="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1550751827-4bd374c3f58b?q=80&w=2070&auto=format&fit=crop" 
                status="PENDING" 
              />
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* Arsenal Quick Access */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-2">
            <ArsenalButton icon={<Activity size={18} />} label="Analytics" />
            <ArsenalButton icon={<Zap size={18} />} label="Arsenal" />
          </div>

        </main>

        {/* --- Bottom Nav Bar --- */}
        <nav className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 bg-[#102216]/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-t border-white/10 px-8 py-4 pb-8 max-w-md mx-auto">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center relative">
            <NavIcon icon={<LayoutGrid size={24} />} label="DASH" active={activeTab === "DASH"} onClick={() => setActiveTab("DASH")} />
            <NavIcon icon={<Search size={24} />} label="MAP" active={activeTab === "MAP"} onClick={() => setActiveTab("MAP")} />
            
            {/* Pulsing Action Button */}
            <div className="relative -top-8">
              <motion.button 
                whileHover={{ scale: 1.1 }}
                whileTap={{ scale: 0.9 }}
                className="bg-[#0df259] text-black p-4 rounded-full shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(13,242,89,0.5)] border-8 border-[#102216]"
              >
                <Sparkles className="w-8 h-8" />
              </motion.button>
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[#0df259] rounded-full blur-[20px] opacity-20 -z-10 animate-pulse"></div>
            </div>

            <NavIcon icon={<MessageCircle size={24} />} label="COMM" active={activeTab === "COMM"} onClick={() => setActiveTab("COMM")} />
            <NavIcon icon={<User size={24} />} label="ID" active={activeTab === "ID"} onClick={() => setActiveTab("ID")} />
          </div>
        </nav>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// --- Internal Components ---

function MetricCard({ label, value, trend, icon, isPercent = false }: any) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white/5 border border-white/5 rounded-2xl p-5 flex flex-col items-center text-center group hover:border-[#0df259]/30 transition-all overflow-hidden relative">
      <div className="absolute -right-4 -top-4 w-12 h-12 bg-[#0df259]/10 rounded-full blur-xl group-hover:bg-[#0df259]/20 transition-all"></div>
      
      <div className="relative size-16 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full border-4 border-white/5 border-t-[#0df259] shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5)]">
         <div className="absolute -top-1 right-0">{icon}</div>
         <p className="text-white text-2xl font-black font-mono tracking-tighter drop-shadow-[0_0_5px_rgba(13,242,89,0.5)]">
           {value}{isPercent && <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-bold ml-0.5">%</span>}
         </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-3 space-y-0.5">
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-[8px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">{label}</p>
        <p className={cn("text-[9px] font-black font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full", trend.includes('+') ? "text-[#0df259] bg-[#0df259]/10" : "text-slate-400 bg-white/5")}>{trend}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function MatchCard({ title, time, img, status }: any) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white/5 group flex items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl p-3 pr-4 border border-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border-l-4 border-l-transparent hover:border-l-[#0df259] transition-all">
      <div className="relative h-14 w-14 shrink-0 rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-white/10">
        <img src={img} className="h-full w-full object-cover group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform duration-700 brightness-75" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/60 to-transparent"></div>
      </div>
      
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
        <h4 className="text-white text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-tight truncate group-hover:text-[#0df259] transition-colors">{title}</h4>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 opacity-50 mt-0.5">
          <Calendar size={10} className="text-[#0df259]" />
          <p className="text-[9px] font-black font-mono truncate">{time}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <button className={cn(
        "h-10 px-5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black font-mono tracking-widest transition-all",
        status === "ACCEPT" ? "bg-[#0df259] text-black shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(13,242,89,0.3)] hover:scale-105" : "bg-transparent border border-white/20 text-white hover:border-[#0df259] hover:text-[#0df259]"
      )}>
        {status}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function ArsenalButton({ icon, label }: any) {
  return (
    <button className="bg-white/5 p-4 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center gap-2 border border-white/5 group hover:bg-[#0df259]/10 hover:border-[#0df259]/30 transition-all active:scale-95">
      <div className="p-3 rounded-full bg-white/5 group-hover:bg-[#0df259] group-hover:text-black transition-all">
        {icon}
      </div>
      <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 group-hover:text-white">{label}</span>
    </button>
  );
}

function NavIcon({ icon, label, active = false, onClick }: any) {
  return (
    <button 
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col items-center gap-1 transition-all",
        active ? "text-[#0df259]" : "text-white/30 hover:text-white"
      )}
    >
      {React.cloneElement(icon, { size: 24, className: active ? "stroke-[3px]" : "stroke-[2px]" })}
      <span className="text-[8px] font-black tracking-widest mt-1">{label}</span>
    </button>
  );
}

$files = Get-ChildItem -Path src -Filter "*.tsx" -Recurse
foreach ($file in $files) {
    $content = Get-Content $file.FullName
    $imports = $content | Select-String -Pattern "from\s+['""](@/|(\.{1,2}/)+)([^'""\s]+)['""]"
    foreach ($match in $imports.Matches) {
        $path = $match.Groups[3].Value
        # Resolver alias @/
        if ($match.Groups[1].Value -eq "@/") {
            $fullPath = Join-Path "src" $path
        } else {
            $dir = Split-Path $file.FullName
            $fullPath = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $dir $path))
        }
        
        # Aadir extensin si falta
        if (!($fullPath -match "\.(tsx|ts|js|jsx)$")) {
            $testTsx = $fullPath + ".tsx"
            $testTs = $fullPath + ".ts"
            if (!(Test-Path $testTsx) -and !(Test-Path $testTs)) {
                # Comprobar si es un directorio con index.tsx
                $testIndex = Join-Path $fullPath "index.tsx"
                if (!(Test-Path $testIndex)) {
                     Write-Host "BLOQUEO DETECTADO: El archivo '$path' importado en '$($file.Name)' NO EXISTE." -ForegroundColor Red
                }
            }
        } elseif (!(Test-Path $fullPath)) {
             Write-Host "BLOQUEO DETECTADO: El archivo '$path' importado en '$($file.Name)' NO EXISTE." -ForegroundColor Red
        }
    }
}`*
